json.loads(line) for line in source.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n        ]\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _write_rows(source: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> None:\n        source.write_text(\n            \"\".join(\n                json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(\",\", \":\")) + \"\\n\"\n                for row in rows\n            ),\n            encoding=\"utf-8\",\n        )\n\n    def _refused(\n        self, code: str, message_regex: str, source: Path, root: Path, **overrides\n    ):\n        kwargs = {\n            \"source_root\": overrides.pop(\"source_root\", root),\n            \"exported_at_utc\": overrides.pop(\"exported_at_utc\", EXPORTED_AT),\n        }\n        target = overrides.pop(\"target\", root / \"out.jsonl\")\n        with self.assertRaisesRegex(subject.ExportRefused, message_regex) as caught:\n            subject.export_partition(source, target, **kwargs)\n        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, between the exists-check and the move must not be\n        # replaced. The race is simulated by making the target's exists-check answer False while\n        # the target really exists; on Windows os.rename refuses (no-clobber, nothing unlinked).\n        if os.name != \"nt\":\n            self.skipTest(\"documented residual window on POSIX (see _publish)\")\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:\n            root = Path(temporary)\n            source_root, source = self._partition(root / \"window\")\n            target = root / \"out\" / \"partition.jsonl\"\n            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True)\n            foreign = b'{\"foreign\": true}\\n'\n            target.write_bytes(foreign)\n            unchanged_exists = Path.exists\n\n            def target_looks_absent(path):\n                if path == target:\n                    return False\n                return unchanged_exists(path)\n\n            with (\n                mock.patch.object(\n                    Path, \"exists\", autospec=True, side_effect=target_looks_absent\n                ),\n                self.assertRaisesRegex(\n                    subject.ExportRefused, \"appeared before publication\"\n                ) as caught,\n            ):\n                subject.export_partition(\n                    source,\n                    target,\n                    source_root=source_root,\n                    exported_at_utc=EXPORTED_AT,\n                )\n            self.assertEqual(caught.exception.code, \"target_exists\")\n            self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), foreign)\n            self.assertTrue(Path(str(target) + subject.STAGING_SUFFIX).exists())\n            self.assertTrue(Path(str(target) + \".p030export.json\").exists())\n\n    def T0 review finding 1: an ENOSPC-shaped failure after the file was created must not\n        leave a truncated partition under the target name (the adapter would accept it).\"\"\"\n        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:\n            root = Path(temporary)\n            source_root, source = self._partition(root)\n            target = root / \"out.jsonl\"\n            staging_target = Path(str(target) + subject.STAGING_SUFFIX)\n            original_open = Path.open\n\n            def half_then_enospc(path, *args, **kwargs):\n                handle = original_open(path, *args, **kwargs)\n                if path == staging_target and args and \"x\" in args[0]:\n                    real_write = handle.write\n\n                    def write(data):\n                        real_write(data[: len(data) // 2])\n                        raise OSError(28, \"No space left on device\")\n\n                    handle.write = write\n                return handle\n\n            with mock.patch.object(Path, \"open\", half_then_enospc):\n                self._refused(\n                    \"target_unwritable\",\n                    \"nothing was self.assertFalse(target.exists())\n            self.assertFalse(Path(str(target) + \".p030export.json\").exists())\n            # the half-written bytes exist only under the staging name, which is self.assertLess(staging_target.stat().st_size, source.stat().st_size)\n            # a second run refuses on the leftover instead of adopting or removing it\n            self._refused(\n self._partition(root)\n            module = Path(subject.__file__)\n            lf_digest = hashlib.sha256(\n                module.read_bytes().replace(b\"\\r\\n\", b\"\\n\")\n            ).hexdigest()\n            receipt = subject.export_partition(\n                source,\n                root / \"a.jsonl\",\n                source_root=source_root,\n
